#### Ibrahim Alfa Jnr — Ikoyi — provisional ADD / retained discovery

### Canonical evaluations

Before Stadium Drive, the track's complete ratchet-to-drop arrival would be followed by Stadium Drive's documented ambience-to-broken-techno formation, creating two consecutive beginnings before Marauder anchors the opening. Between Stadium Drive and Marauder, the same complete arrival interrupts rather than extends the already established formation.
- **Long-form journey:** FAILS THESE PLACEMENTS. The 3:21 duration is proportionally modest, but the journey already has a transformation-led overture and an eight-track opening third. A second full arrival adds explanation rather than a missing direction.
- **Verdict:** placement-specific **REJECT** for the two Run 101 opening positions only. Retained as an exact interesting discovery.

#### Bitchin Bajas — Ajami — placement-specific REJECT / retained discovery

- **Volume fit:** PASS at the adjacent edge. Pitchfork documents steady kalimba pulses becoming lilting 6/8 under a triplet bassline and later returning to a 4/4 motorik stomp.

- **Surrounding three-to-five-track run:** FAILS THESE PLACEMENTS. At the central boundary, Ajami would fully narrate 6/8-to-4/4 reorientation before the existing slower stagger and broken-beat deformation.
- **Long-form journey:** FAILS THESE PLACEMENTS. Its scale is musically meaningful, not automatically excessive, but neither proposed position gives that scale a consequence unavailable in the current journey.
- **Verdict:** placement-specific **REJECT** for the two Run 101 positions only. Retained as an exact interesting discovery.

#### Bitchin Bajas — Isle Peaks — PARKED / retained discovery

- **Volume fit:** PLAUSIBLE only at the adjacent boundary. Pitchfork documents a freeform path through field and natural sounds, needling drone, strummed strings and a final pitch dive, but no track-specific rhythmic or techno premise.
- **Immediate transition:** UNKNOWN before Stadium Drive. The final pitch movement may make the following rhythmic formation more consequential or may simply delay it.
- **Surrounding three-to-five-track run:** DECISION-CRITICAL UNKNOWN. The proposed `Isle Peaks → Stadium Drive → Marauder → The Witching Hour` opening could read as elemental prehistory becoming rhythm, but the available exact-track evidence cannot distinguish that from ten minutes of unrelated prelude.
- **Long-form journey:** NOT RESPONSIBLY CHOSEN. The track's 10:00 duration and conspicuous production-world change are not defects. The blocker is the absence of a track-specific bridge from its documented freeform path into the volume's techno premise.
- **Verdict:** **PARKED**. Reopen only for materially richer exact-track evidence or a changed opening need; no listening task is assigned.

### Canonical evaluations

#### Peverelist — Pulse I — ADD

- **Volume fit:** PASS. Resident Advisor describes the exact track as quietly inventive broken techno with crooked formation, percussive clacks / woodblock-style percussion and a gradual introduction of skeletal new elements. This is track-specific rhythmic and structural evidence, not novelty as a substitute for fit.
- **Immediate transition:** PASS provisionally at `Tehutis Law → Pulse I → Fragments`. The measured working path is `139 → 136 → 140 BPM`, with the independent 133-BPM conflict preserved. Close tempo is supporting evidence only. The transition-scale purpose is explicit: after Tehutis Law's provisional re-grounding, Pulse I introduces a crooked accumulation stage before Fragments' provisional driving re-escalation. Entry, exit, phrasing and live-mix feel remain unknown, but the repository does not require direct audio proof for a provisional placement when the best lawful evidence supports a deliberate transition-scale function.
- **Surrounding three-to-five-track run:** PASS provisionally. The local run becomes `Thick to Handle → Tehutis Law → Pulse I → Fragments → Drummz`: provisional deformation → re-grounding → crooked accumulation → re-escalation → restless hinge. Those role labels are editorial interpretation, not measured sonic facts. The addition gives the middle passage a legible developmental step rather than inserting a track merely because it is unusual.
- **Long-form journey:** PASS provisionally. The 6:50 insertion expands the middle without touching either listener-protected handoff and without defaulting growth to the tail. Its documented gradual construction supplies a journey function distinct from simply demonstrating another groove or production world.
- **Verdict:** **ADD** between Jayson Wynters — Tehutis Law and CHANCEKNOT — Fragments. Provisional; audio and live mixing remain untested.

#### Jump Source — A Dull Knife (feat. Harmony Index) — placement-specific REJECT / retained discovery

- **Volume fit:** PASS at the adjacent edge. Resident Advisor describes the exact track beginning as a dub-techno groover and growing into warm syncopated bass music when Harmony Index enters. The internal transformation is genuinely useful discovery evidence.
- **Immediate transition:** FAIL for the two tested placements only. Exact 120 BPM produces measured `Cave 134 → candidate 120 → Too Much Time 140` and `Truth in Noise 138 → candidate 120 → Transparency 143*` paths. BPM is not energy or flow and a hard turn can pass; here, however, the available evidence does not explain why either conspicuous tempo reset improves those exact boundaries.
- **Surrounding three-to-five-track run:** FAIL for the proposed placements. In the early run the 120-BPM insertion would sit inside `136 → 136 → 134 → 120 → 140 → 140`; before the protected trio it would create `Drummz 138 → Truth in Noise 138 → 120 → Transparency 143* → When We Froze 140`. These numbers do not prove bad flow, but without track-specific evidence connecting the reset to either local run, the placement purpose is weaker than the disruption it introduces.
- **Long-form journey:** NOT PROVEN for these placements. The different-world transformation remains interesting, but this run does not establish a net journey gain at either tested boundary.
- **Verdict:** **REJECT the two Run 86 placements only**, not the track globally. Retain locally and in the doctrine-free global utility playlist as genuinely interesting.

`*` Transparency's 143/107 BPM conflict remains preserved; the placement verdict does not rely on treating 143 as perceived pulse.

The unresolved `Suspension of Disbelief` lead receives no ADD/REJECT/REVISIT verdict.

**APPROVED.** The immutable schemaVersion 2 request is pinned to the live pre-request SHA and the matching PARTIAL snapshot is internally valid: runId, sourceCommit, mode and target match; 3 requested = 2 selected exact resolutions + 1 unresolved; the exact resolver error and Pulse I warning are preserved; no stale candidate, duplicate, substitute or alternate entered evaluation. Pulse I's 136/133 metadata conflict is preserved. The protected `Transparency → When We Froze → Stardancer` internal order remains untouched. Auditor approves the Pulse I ADD, the two-placement A Dull Knife rejection, local inspiration retention, and two global utility additions.

### Durable result

- STRANGE GAIT: **14 → 15 canonical tracks**; add Peverelist — Pulse I after Tehutis Law and before Fragments; no other reorder.
- Protected internal order remains `Transparency → When We Froze → Stardancer`.
- Local inspiration: Pulse I exact canonical ADD; A Dull Knife exact placement-specific REJECT / interesting; Suspension of Disbelief unresolved research lead; current Orbe Records and Repetitive Rhythm Research release windows retained as watches without invented track facts.

**RUN ANALYSIS:** Approximately 34 inspected → 3 ranked leads → 2 exact resolutions + 1 unresolved → 2 evaluations → **1 ADD + 1 placement-specific REJECT** → 2 utility additions. The pre-run stagnation count was **31 consecutive runs without a canonical ADD**, last ADD Run 54. Run 86 ends that drought; it is the first ADD in 32 runs counting Runs 55–86. The important workflow finding is not that the bar should be looser: repeated prior evaluations had effectively promoted missing direct handoff audio from an uncertainty into a veto, even though the repository permits provisional best-evidence sequencing. A Dull Knife demonstrates the opposite: interesting exact discovery value does not override an unsupported placement. The run does not prove Pulse I's live transition or phrase fit, does not globally reject A Dull Knife, and does not prove STRANGE GAIT is complete.
